\title{Melanie-ANALOGY — King's Indian, Fianchetto}

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{O-O}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{O-O} (\texttt{e1g1}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#2949} (ply 15) [structural distance $d$=28, QAP obj=11]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{n}f6 as their \emph{B}c8, our \emph{B}c1 as their \emph{P}a7; transferred move \texttt{e1g1} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#2950} (ply 15) [structural distance $d$=28, QAP obj=11]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{n}f6 as their \emph{B}c8, our \emph{B}c1 as their \emph{P}a7; transferred move \texttt{e1g1} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0001\#808} (ply 14) [structural distance $d$=42, QAP obj=8]: the master played \texttt{O-O}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Ke1} plays the role of that game's \emph{Ke1}; cross-type roles: our \emph{b}g7 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{p}d6 as their \emph{B}c1; transferred move \texttt{e1g1} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 45 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{O-O} (summed QAP objective 338, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
